Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in mathematical_statistics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 253 and the invariant I(A,B) = 570 mod 5.

Theorem 213.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: Let the mathematical_statistics structure S satisfy the conditions of conservation law framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 484. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 996 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).
